## 角度约束

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 约束两条直线的夹角为给定的角度(弧度制)值

**示例：**

```python
#角度约束：直线-直线
pt1 = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
vec1 = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
vec2 = NCTI.Vector(1,1,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","bline1", pt1, vec1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","bline2", pt1, vec2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bver", pt1)
#create dcm 
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmline1", "dcmsys1", pt1, vec1, "bline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmline2", "dcmsys1", pt1, vec2, "bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt", "dcmsys1", pt1, "bver")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "dcmline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "dcmpt")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmcoin", DCM_COINCIDENT)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "dcmcoin", "dcmline2", "dcmpt")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmangle", DCM_ANGLE, 2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "dcmangle", "dcmline1", "dcmline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
doc.Rename("bline1","result1")
doc.Rename("bline2","result2")
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ["result1", "result2"]]
print(delete_list)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

## 设置B样条或贝塞尔曲线权重

**类型：** 基础命令

**说明：** 设置B样条或贝塞尔曲线权重

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_set_bezier_bspline_weight", strObjName, iUIdx, iVIdx, iWeight)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_bspline_curve", "bs", 
 2, 
 [0.0,1.0], 
 [3,3], 
 [NCTI.Point(0,0,0),
 NCTI.Point(0.5,1,0),
 NCTI.Point(1,0,0)], 
 [1.0,1.0,1.0])
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_set_bezier_bspline_weight", "bs", 1, 0.5)
```

---

## 设置公差

**类型：** 基础命令

**说明：** 设置模型的容差值

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_set_tolerance", strObjName, dTolce)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b1", 10, 10, 10)
# 设置长方体"b1"的容差值为0.001
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_set_tolerance", "b1", 0.001)
```

---

## 设置对齐方式

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 设置对齐方式

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm3_dim_alignment", strDimName, justification)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_dim_sys", "dcmsys")
pt = NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0)
dir = NCTI.Vector(10, 10, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "line1", pt, dir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_line", "dcm_line1","dcmsys", pt, dir, "line1")
pt = NCTI.Point(10, 10, 0)
dir = NCTI.Vector(10, 5, 5)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "line2", pt, dir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_line", "dcm_line2","dcmsys", pt, dir, "line2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_dimension", "dcm_par", DCM3_PARALLEL)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm3_addd", "dcmsys", "dcm_par", "dcm_line1", "dcm_line2")
#设置对齐方式
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm3_dim_alignment", "dcm_par", DCM3_ALIGNMENT_ALIGN)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm3_evaluate", "dcmsys")
```

---

## 设置执行约束求解时自动添加约束维度的维度类别

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据约束维度对象名称，选择对齐方式和对齐类型，设置约束尺寸对齐方式，设置执行约束求解时自动添加约束维度的维度类别

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", strDcmSysName, dcmGeoType, dcmGeoType, dcmDimType, enumType)`

**示例：**

```python
def report_balance(dimSysName):
    con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance(dimSysName)
    res = con_bal_res[1] - con_bal_res[2] - con_bal_res[3];
    if res < 0:
        print(f"Overconstrained by {res}")
    elif res == 0:
        print("Right number of dimensions")
    else:
        print(f"Underconstrained by {res}")
#create model body
ptOrigin = NCTI.Point(0, 0,0)
xdir = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
ydir = NCTI.Vector(0,1,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","BlineX", ptOrigin, xdir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","BlineY", ptOrigin, ydir)
pt1 = NCTI.Point(10, 10,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(10, 15,0)
pt3 = NCTI.Point(20, 10,0)
dir = NCTI.Vector(2,-1,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline1", pt1, xdir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline2", pt2, ydir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline3", pt3, dir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","Bpt1", pt1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","Bpt2", pt2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","Bpt3", pt3)
#Create DCM
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
#Create fixed vertical and horizontal reference lines
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "DlineX", "dcmsys1", ptOrigin, xdir, "BlineX")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "DlineY", "dcmsys1", ptOrigin, ydir, "BlineY")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "DlineX")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "DlineY")
#Add three more lines to the DCM
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline1", "dcmsys1", pt1, xdir, "Bline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline2", "dcmsys1", pt2, ydir, "Bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline3", "dcmsys1", pt3, dir, "Bline3")
#Add points and make coincident with lines
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "coi", DCM_COINCIDENT, 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt1", "dcmsys1", pt1, "Bpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt2", "dcmsys1", pt2, "Bpt2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt3", "dcmsys1", pt3, "Bpt3")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "coi", "Dpt1", "Dline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "coi", "Dpt1", "Dline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "coi", "Dpt2", "Dline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "coi", "Dpt2", "Dline3")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "coi", "Dpt3", "Dline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "coi", "Dpt3", "Dline3")
#Disable non-algebraic dimensioning schemes
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_set_option", "dcmsys1", DCM_OPKEY_SOLVE_NON_ALGEBRAIC, DCM_OPVAL_OFF)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
print("Before autodimension:")
report_balance("dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", "dcmsys1", DCM_ADG_LINE, DCM_ADG_LINE,DCM_ADD_ANGLE, DCM_ADM_ALL)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
print("After adding angle dimensions:")
report_balance("dcmsys1")
```

---

## 设置控制其他功能行为的选项

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据约束系统对象名称，选择的键与值，设置控制其他功能行为的选项

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_set_option", strDcmSysName, key, value)`

**示例：**

```python
#Angle_AngleLine_A1
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys")
origin = NCTI.Point(0.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "bLine", origin, NCTI.Vector(0.100000, 0.000000, 0.000000))
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmLine", "dcmsys", origin, NCTI.Vector(0.100000, 0.000000, 0.000000),
               "bLine")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys", "dcmLine", 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "bLine2", origin, NCTI.Vector(0.000000, 0.100000, 0.000000))
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmLine2", "dcmsys", origin, NCTI.Vector(0.000000, 0.100000, 0.000000),
               "bLine2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys", "dcmLine2", 0)
vpoint = NCTI.Point(0.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000)
vpoint2 = NCTI.Point(1.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000)
vvector = NCTI.Vector(1.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", "bVertex", vpoint)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmPoint", "dcmsys", vpoint, "bVertex")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", "bVertex2", vpoint2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmPoint2", "dcmsys", vpoint2, "bVertex2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "bLine3", vpoint, vvector)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmLine3", "dcmsys", vpoint, vvector, "bLine3")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys", "dcmLine3", 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmCons", DCM_COINCIDENT)
dData = NCTI.AdddData()
dData.m_GNodeNameTwo = "dcmLine3"
dData.m_GNodeNameOne = "dcmPoint"
dData.m_DNodeName = "d_node"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys", "dcmCons", dData)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmCons2", DCM_COINCIDENT)
dData2 = NCTI.AdddData()
dData2.m_GNodeNameTwo = "dcmLine3"
dData2.m_GNodeNameOne = "dcmPoint2"
dData2.m_DNodeName = "d_node2"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys", "dcmCons2", dData2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys")
vpoint3 = NCTI.Point(0.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000)
vpoint4 = NCTI.Point(1.000000, 2.000000, 0.000000)
vvector2 = NCTI.Vector(1.000000, 2.000000, 0.000000)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", "bVertex3", vpoint3)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmPoint3", "dcmsys", vpoint3, "bVertex3")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys", "dcmPoint3", 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", "bVertex4", vpoint4)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmPoint4", "dcmsys", vpoint4, "bVertex4")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "bLine4", vpoint3, vvector2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmLine4", "dcmsys", vpoint3, vvector2, "bLine4")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmCons3", DCM_COINCIDENT)
dData3 = NCTI.AdddData()
dData3.m_GNodeNameTwo = "dcmLine4"
dData3.m_GNodeNameOne = "dcmPoint3"
dData3.m_DNodeName = "d_node3"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys", "dcmCons3", dData3)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmCons4", DCM_COINCIDENT)
dData4 = NCTI.AdddData()
dData4.m_GNodeNameTwo = "dcmLine4"
dData4.m_GNodeNameOne = "dcmPoint4"
dData4.m_DNodeName = "d_node4"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys", "dcmCons4", dData4)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmCons5", DCM_ANGLE, 0.000000)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_alignment", "dcmCons5", 0, DCM_ALIGNMENT_ANTI_ALIGN)
dData5 = NCTI.AdddData()
dData5.m_GNodeNameTwo = "dcmLine4"
dData5.m_GNodeNameOne = "dcmLine3"
dData5.m_DNodeName = "d_node5"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys", "dcmCons5", dData5)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys")
def report_balance(dimSysName):
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_set_option", dimSysName, DCM_OPKEY_SOLVE_NON_ALGEBRAIC, DCM_OPVAL_OFF)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_LINE, DCM_ADG_LINE, DCM_ADD_ANGLE,
                   DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_SPLINE, DCM_ADG_SPLINE, DCM_ADD_ANGLE,
                   DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_SPLINE, DCM_ADG_SPLINE, DCM_ADD_DISTANCE,
                   DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    # Auto-dimension - add distance dimensions
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_ALL, DCM_ADG_ALL, DCM_ADD_DISTANCE,
                   DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_CIRCLE, DCM_ADG_CIRCLE, DCM_ADD_RADIUS,
                   DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_ELLIPSE, DCM_ADG_ELLIPSE,
                   DCM_ADD_MAJOR_RADIUS, DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_autodimension_mask", dimSysName, DCM_ADG_ELLIPSE, DCM_ADG_ELLIPSE,
                   DCM_ADD_MINOR_RADIUS, DCM_ADM_ALL)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_autodimension", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", dimSysName)
    doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_set_option", dimSysName, DCM_OPKEY_SOLVE_NON_ALGEBRAIC, DCM_OPVAL_ON)
    con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance(dimSysName)
    d_f = con_bal_res[1]
    c_f = con_bal_res[2]
    if d_f == c_f:
        print(f"**Free dimensions{d_f} equal to constraint dimensions: {c_f} **")
    else:
        print(f"**Free dimensions{d_f} not equal to constraint dimensions: {c_f}**")
    res = con_bal_res[1] - con_bal_res[2] - con_bal_res[3];
    if res < 0:
        print(f"Overconstrained by {res}")
    elif res == 0:
        print("Right number of dimensions")
    else:
        print(f"Underconstrained by {res}")
report_balance("dcmsys")
doc.Rename("bLine2", "result1")
doc.Rename("bLine", "result2")
doc.Rename("bLine3", "result3")
doc.Rename("bLine4", "result4")
doc.Rename("bVertex3", "result5")
doc.Rename("bVertex4", "result6")
doc.Rename("bVertex2", "result7")
doc.Rename("bVertex", "result8")
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if
               item not in ["result1", "result2", "result3", "result4", "result5", "result6", "result7", "result8"]]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

## 设置相同参数

**类型：** 基础命令

**说明：** 设置每个边的各种表示，对于相同的参数能够给出一致的3D点位置

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_same_parameter", strObjName, dMaxTol, "-force")`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b1", NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0), 10, 20, 30)
max_tol = 0.01
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_same_parameter", "b1", max_tol, "-force")
```

---

## 设置约束对象对应的节点置空

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据约束系统对象名称、约束对象类型，将约束对象对应的节点置空，用于错误用例

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_set_null_dcm_node", strDcmObjectName, strDcmSysName, dcmObjectType)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
pt1 = NCTI.Point(1,1,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bver1", pt1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt1", "dcmsys1", pt1, "bver1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_set_null_dcm_node", "dcmpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
print("Constraint object corresponding node null setting successful.")
```

---

## 设置约束尺寸对象是否保持刚性

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据约束尺寸对象名称，设置约束尺寸对象是否保持刚性

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_set_rigidity", strDimName, dcmRigidityEnum)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
pt1 = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(5,0,0)
pt3 = NCTI.Point(10,0,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", "Bver1", pt1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", "Bver2", pt2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt1", "dcmsys1", pt1, "Bver1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt2", "dcmsys1", pt2, "Bver2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dist1", DCM_DISTANCE, 6)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_setends", "dist1", "Dpt1", "Dpt2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_set_rigidity", "dist1", DCM_DIMENSION_RIGID)
print("Set rigidity successful.")
```

---

## 设置约束尺寸对齐方式

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据约束维度对象名称，选择对齐方式和对齐类型，设置约束尺寸对齐方式

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_alignment", strDimName, justification, alignmentType)`

**示例：**

```python
pt1 = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
vec1 = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","bline1", pt1, vec1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmline1", "dcmsys1", pt1, vec1, "bline1")
pt1 = NCTI.Point(13,6,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_circle","bcir1", pt1, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_circle", "dcmcir1", "dcmsys1", pt1, 10, "bcir1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmtan", DCM_TANGENT, 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "dcmtan", "dcmcir1", "dcmline1")
#设置约束维度对齐方式
#参数个数：4
#param1(string)：命令名称
#param2(string)：约束维度对象名称
#param3(int)：对齐方式
#0：DCM_alignment
#1：DCM_ed_alignment
#2：DCM_tan_alignment
#3：DCM_norm_alignment
#4：DCM_sym_alignment
#param4(int)：对齐类型
#0：DCM_alignment枚举类型：
# DCM_ALIGNMENT_NOT_SPECIFIED,
# DCM_ALIGNMENT_CURRENT,
# DCM_ALIGNMENT_ALIGN,
# DCM_ALIGNMENT_ANTI_ALIGN,
# DCM_ALIGNMENT_CLOCKWISE,
# DCM_ALIGNMENT_ANTICLOCKWISE
#1：DCM_ed_alignment枚举类型：
# DCM_ED_ALIGNMENT_EQUAL,
# DCM_ED_ALIGNMENT_OPPOSITE,
# DCM_ED_ALIGNMENT_CURRENT
#2：DCM_tan_alignment枚举类型：
# DCM_TAN_ALIGNMENT_EQUAL,
# DCM_TAN_ALIGNMENT_OPPOSITE,
# DCM_TAN_ALIGNMENT_CURRENT,
# DCM_TAN_ALIGNMENT_FREE
#3：DCM_norm_alignment枚举类型：
# DCM_NORM_ALIGNMENT_CLOCKWISE,
# DCM_NORM_ALIGNMENT_ANTICLOCKWISE,
# DCM_NORM_ALIGNMENT_CURRENT,
# DCM_NORM_ALIGNMENT_FREE
#4：DCM_sym_alignment枚举类型：
# DCM_SYM_ALIGNMENT_NOT_SPECIFIED,
# DCM_SYM_ALIGNMENT_CURRENT,
# DCM_SYM_ALIGNMENT_ALIGN,
# DCM_SYM_ALIGNMENT_ANTI_ALIGN
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_alignment", "dcmtan", 2, DCM_TAN_ALIGNMENT_FREE)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
```

---

## 设置约束尺寸标注的值

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 对约束维度对象，设置约束尺寸标注的值

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_setvalue", strDimName, dimensionValue)`

**示例：**

```python
pt2 = NCTI.Point(0,-50,0)
pt3 = NCTI.Point(15,5,0)
vec1 = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","bline2", pt2, vec1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bpt1", pt3)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmline2", "dcmsys1", pt2, vec1, "bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "dcmline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt1", "dcmsys1", pt3, "bpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmdist", DCM_DISTANCE, 65)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "dcmdist", "dcmline2", "dcmpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_setvalue", "dcmdist", 30)
#Evaluate - will not solve with default circle-inversion
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1", 1)
```

---

## 设置线性方程的变量系数

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据约束系统对象名称，线性方程对象名称，方程中第n个变量的索引，变量对象名称，变量的系数值，系数名称标识，设置线性方程的变量系数

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_linear_setcoeff", strDcmSysName, lineEquationObjectName, index, varObjectName, varCoefficientValue, coefficientNameSign)`

**示例：**

```python
#create model body
pt1 = NCTI.Point(34,-23,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(34,43,0)
pt3 = NCTI.Point(23,31,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_circle","Bcir1", pt1, 14)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","Bpt1", pt2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","Bpt2", pt3)
#create dcm
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
#create circle and add to the DCM
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_circle", "Dcir1", "dcmsys1", pt1, 14, "Bcir1")
#create radius dimension for circle and add to the DCM
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "rad", DCM_RADIUS, 30)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "rad", "Dcir1", "r_node_rad")
#create two points
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt1", "dcmsys1", pt2, "Bpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "Dpt2", "dcmsys1", pt3, "Bpt2")
#create distance dimension between the two points
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dist", DCM_DISTANCE, 20)
adata = NCTI.AdddData()
adata.m_GNodeNameOne = 'Dpt1'
adata.m_GNodeNameTwo = "Dpt2"
adata.m_DNodeName = "d_node_dist"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "dist", adata)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_variable", "var1", "dcmsys1", 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_variable", "var2", "dcmsys1", 16.1, "d_node_dist", 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_variable", "var3", "dcmsys1", 34.3,  "r_node_rad", 1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_linear_equation", "lin1", "dcmsys1", 2, 59)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_linear_setcoeff", "dcmsys1", "lin1", 0, "var1", 3, 1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_linear_setcoeff", "dcmsys1", "lin1", 1, "var2", 4, 2)
print("The value of var1 is set to 3")
print("The value of var2 is set to 4")
```

---

## 设置阵列水平和竖直方向的元素数量

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 根据尺寸约束对象名称，x方向数量、y方向数量，设置阵列水平和竖直方向的元素数量

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_set_pattern_plier", strDimName, xDirQuantity, yDirQuantity)`

**示例：**

```python
#create model body
geom_list = []
pt = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
xdir = NCTI.Vector(1, 0, 0)
ydir = NCTI.Vector(0, 1, 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline1", pt, xdir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline2", pt, ydir)
geom_list.append("Bline1")
geom_list.append("Bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline1", "dcmsys1", pt, xdir, "Bline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline2", "dcmsys1", pt, ydir, "Bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "Dline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "Dline2")
# Add a 2D pattern
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_pattern_2d", "patt_2d_gn","dcmsys1", "Dline1", "Dline2")
# Define pattern sizes
xsize = 8
ysize = 5
# Create arrays of points and circles
ptss = []
cirss = []
for ix in range(xsize):
    pts = []
    cirs = []
    for iy in range(ysize):
        BptName = f"Bpt{ix}{iy}"
        DptName = f"Dpt{ix}{iy}"
        pt = NCTI.Point(ix * 30, iy * 15, 0)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", BptName, pt)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", DptName, "dcmsys1", pt, BptName)
        pts.append(DptName)
        geom_list.append(BptName)
        BcirName = f"Bcir{ix}{iy}"
        DcirName = f"Dcir{ix}{iy}"
        r = 5 + (ix + iy) / 2.0
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_circle",BcirName, pt, r)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_circle", DcirName, "dcmsys1", pt, r, BcirName)
        cirs.append(DcirName)
        geom_list.append(BcirName)
    ptss.append(pts)
    cirss.append(cirs)
# Add a concentric between the first point and circle
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "conc", DCM_CONCENTRIC)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_setends", "conc", ptss[0][0], cirss[0][0])
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "conc", ptss[0][0], cirss[0][0])
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", cirss[0][0])
# Add a pattern constraint between the element[0,0] and every other element
for ix in range(xsize):
    for iy in range(ysize):
        dimName = f"pattern{ix}{iy}"
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", dimName, DCM_PATTERNED_2D)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_set_pattern_plier", dimName, ix, iy)
        if ix == 0 and iy == 0:
            continue
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  dimName,ptss[0][0], ptss[ix][iy], "patt_2d_gn")
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  dimName,cirss[0][0], cirss[ix][iy], "patt_2d_gn")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
pvs = doc.dcm_pattern_2d_value("dcmsys1", "patt_2d_gn")
print(pvs)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "pat_value1", DCM_PATTERN_2D_VALUE_1, 40)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "pat_value2", DCM_PATTERN_2D_VALUE_2, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "pat_value1", "patt_2d_gn")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "pat_value2", "patt_2d_gn")
pvs = doc.dcm_pattern_2d_value("dcmsys1", "patt_2d_gn")
print(pvs)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
pvs = doc.dcm_pattern_2d_value("dcmsys1", "patt_2d_gn")
print(pvs)
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
index = 1
result_list = []
for geom in geom_list:
    result_name = f"result{index}"
    doc.Rename(geom, result_name)
    result_list.append(result_name)
    index+=1
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in result_list]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

## 设置面的对齐方式

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 约束求解

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm3_dim_surforient", strDimName, faceNormalAlignmentMethod)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_dim_sys", "dcmsys")
pt = NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b1", pt, 20, 30, 10)
pt = NCTI.Point(10,15,10)
n = NCTI.Vector(0,0,1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_plane", "dcm_p1","dcmsys", pt,n, "b1")
pt = NCTI.Point(50,0,0)
n = NCTI.Vector(0,1,0)
u = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
r = 10
h = 20
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_cylinder", "cyd1", r, h, pt, n, u)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_plane", "dcm_p2","dcmsys", pt,n, "cyd1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm3_dimension", "dcm_coinc", DCM3_COINCIDENT)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm3_addd", "dcmsys", "dcm_coinc", "dcm_p1", "dcm_p2")
#设置面的对齐方式
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm3_dim_surforient", "dcm_coinc", DCM3_SURFACE_ORIENTATION_ALIGN)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm3_evaluate", "dcmsys")
```

## 贝塞尔曲线

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 根据控制点和权重确定一条贝赛尔曲线

**示例：**

```python
pt1 = NCTI.Point(0, -10, 0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(-5, 8, 0)
pt3 = NCTI.Point(10, -3, 0)
pt4 = NCTI.Point(15, 10, 0)
pts = [pt1, pt2, pt3, pt4]
weights = [3, 6, 6, 3]
#由控制点列表创建贝赛尔曲线
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_bezier_curve", "b1", pts)
#由控制点列表、控制点权重列表创建贝赛尔曲线
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_bezier_curve", "b2", pts, weights)
```

---

## 贝塞尔曲面

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 根据控制点和权重创建一个贝赛尔曲面

**示例：**

```python
#由控制点矩阵创建贝塞尔曲面
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_bezier_surf", "bs1", 
	[[NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0), NCTI.Point(10, 0, 5), NCTI.Point(20, 0, 0)], 
	[NCTI.Point(0, 10, 2), NCTI.Point(10, 10, 3), NCTI.Point(20, 10, 2)], 
	[NCTI.Point(0, 20, 10), NCTI.Point(10, 20, 20), NCTI.Point(20, 20, 10)], 
	[NCTI.Point(0, 30, 0), NCTI.Point(10, 30, 0), NCTI.Point(20, 30, 0)]])

#由控制点矩阵、权重矩阵创建贝塞尔曲面
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_bezier_surf", "bs2", 
	[[NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0), NCTI.Point(10, 0, 5), NCTI.Point(20, 0, 0)], 
	[NCTI.Point(0, 10, 2), NCTI.Point(10, 10, 3), NCTI.Point(20, 10, 2)], 
	[NCTI.Point(0, 20, 10), NCTI.Point(10, 20, 20), NCTI.Point(20, 20, 10)], 
	[NCTI.Point(0, 30, 0), NCTI.Point(10, 30, 0), NCTI.Point(20, 30, 0)]], 
	[[2, 1, 2], 
	[3, 1, 3], 
	[1, 1, 1], 
	[2, 1, 1]])
```

## 距离约束

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 给定两个几何对象，约束后两个对象的有一定的距离，距离值自定义

**示例：**

```python
#距离约束：点-点
#create model body
pt1 = NCTI.Point(1,1,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(1,2,0)
xdir = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bver1", pt1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bver2", pt2)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line", "Bline1", pt2, xdir)
#create dcm
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt1", "dcmsys1", pt1, "bver1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt2", "dcmsys1", pt2, "bver2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline1", "dcmsys1", pt2, xdir, "Bline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "dcmpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "Dline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmcoin", DCM_COINCIDENT)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "dcmcoin", "dcmpt2", "Dline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmdist", DCM_DISTANCE, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "dcmdist", "dcmpt1", "dcmpt2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
doc.Rename("bver1","result1")
doc.Rename("bver2","result2")
doc.Rename("Bline1","result3")
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ["result1", "result2", "result3"]]
print(delete_list)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

## 轮廓线

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 在指定平面使用移动的点和方向创造一个轮廓线，由成组的指令和值定义动作，可以重复任意次数

**示例：**

```python
#默认XOY平面
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_profile", "p1", "F 0 10 TT 5.88 -8.1 TT -9.51 3.09 T 19.02 0 TT -5.88 -8.1 TT 0 10 WW")
#指定原点
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_profile", "p2", NCTI.Point(0, 1, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 1, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 1), "f 2 0 c 1 180 l 1 t 0 1 d -1 0 c 1 180 l 0.5")
#指定平面（原点、n轴、u轴）
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_profile", "p3", NCTI.Point(0, 1, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 1, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 1), "f 2 0 c 1 180 l 1 t 0 1 d -1 0 c 1 180 l 0.5")
#指定实体上的平面
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "box", 10, 10, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_profile", "p4", "box", 1, "X 15 C 1 90 Y -5 X -7 Y 2 C -1 90 X -8 W")
#生成圆柱上的螺旋线
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_cylinder","cc", 4, 80)
cellList = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("cc", NCTI.Point(0, 4, 40))
#(3pi, 80)表示在展开面系上终点坐标，WW表示首尾不闭合
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_profile", "p5", "cc", cellList[0], "TT 9.42 80 ww")
```

## 边修复

**类型：** 建模命令


**示例：**

```python
doc.Clear()
doc.ResetCaseResult()

pt1 = NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(100, 0, 0)
pt3 = NCTI.Point(200, 50, 0)
pt4 = NCTI.Point(200, 200, 0)
pt5 = NCTI.Point(100, 260, 0)
pt6 = NCTI.Point(0, 260, 0)
pt7 = NCTI.Point(-100, 200, 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_bspline_curve", "spl", 3,  [0, 1, 2], [4, 3, 4], [pt1, pt2, pt3, pt4, pt5, pt6, pt7],[1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1])

edges = doc.FindEdgeByNearestPoint("spl", pt4)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edge_repair", "spl", edges, 1, 0, 0.01)
```

## 返回两个面实体的相交结果

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 返回两个面实体的相交结果

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_find_inter_btw_face", strRetObj, strBaseObj, faceids, iFlag)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b1", 10, 20, 30)
faceid1 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("b1", NCTI.Point(5.000000, 10.000000, 30.000000))[0]
faceid2 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("b1", NCTI.Point(5.000000, 20.000000, 15.000000))[0]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_find_inter_btw_face", "ret", "b1", [faceid1, faceid2], 0)

names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ['ret']]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

## 通用布尔并集

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 与多对象布尔并（multi unit）的区别在于，通用布尔并集得到的结果对象内部仍然保留输入对象的拓扑结构，而multi unit的结果对象内部是去掉了输入对象的拓扑结构，合并的仅是外部结构

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_general_boolean_unit", strRetObj, nameLst)`

**示例：**

```python
#创建一个长方体"b1"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b1", NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0), 10, 10, 10)
#创建一个长方体"b2"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b2", NCTI.Point(-2, -2, 2), 7, 14, 6)
#创建一个长方体"b3"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b3", NCTI.Point(5, -2, 2), 7, 14, 6)
#对长方体"b1"、"b2"、"b3"取通过布尔并集
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_general_boolean_unit", "ret", ['b1', 'b2', 'b3'])
#删除长方体"b1"、"b2"、"b3"
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", ["b1", "b2", "b3"])
```

---

## 通过样条曲线和轮廓演化出新的实体

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 根据给定的样条曲线对象、轮廓对象以及相关参数创建新的几何实体对象

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_evolved_operate", strRetObj, strTagObj, strContObj, iCloseSolid, iVolume, iToComputeAxes)`

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_profile", "p", "F -1 10 TT -1 -1 C 1 90 TT 10 -1 W")
pt1 = NCTI.Point(-1,-1,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(0,-1.5,5)
pts = [pt1, pt2]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_polyline", "t", pts)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_evolved_operate", "ret", "p", "t", 1, 1, 1)
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ['ret']]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

## 通过点扫掠生成螺旋线

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 通过点扫掠生成螺旋线

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_make_helical_curve_by_point", "hc", NCTI.Point(110, 110, 0), NCTI.Point(10, 10, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 1.2), 0, 0.0, 720.0, 100.0, -30.0, 0.01)
```

## 重合约束

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 在平面上创建任意两个点，其中一个点位置不变，约束后两个点在同一坐标位置

**示例：**

```python
#重合约束：点-点
#create model body
pt1 = NCTI.Point(2,5,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(4,7,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bver1", pt1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex","bver2", pt2)
#create dcm
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt1", "dcmsys1", pt1, "bver1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", "dcmpt2", "dcmsys1", pt2, "bver2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "dcmpt1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmcoin", DCM_COINCIDENT)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "dcmcoin", "dcmpt1", "dcmpt2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
doc.Rename("bver1","result1")
doc.Rename("bver2","result2")
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ["result1", "result2"]]
print(delete_list)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

## 锁定约束

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 给定两个对象，对其进行中心锁定约束和全部锁定约束，约束后两个对象的位置和尺寸不会被改变

**示例：**

```python
pt1 = NCTI.Point(0,2,0)
pt2 = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
vec = NCTI.Vector(2,0,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","bline", pt1, vec)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_circle","bcircle", pt2, 1)
#create dcm
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "dcmline", "dcmsys1", pt1, vec, "bline")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_circle", "dcmcircle", "dcmsys1", pt2, 1, "bcircle")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmlock1", DCM_LOCK_CENTRE)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmlock2", DCM_LOCK_WHOLE)
#圆中心锁定
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1", "dcmlock1", "dcmcircle")
#直线全局锁定
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1", "dcmlock2", "dcmline")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmtan", DCM_TANGENT)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1", "dcmtan", "dcmline", "dcmcircle")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
doc.Rename("bline","result1")
doc.Rename("bcircle","result2")
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ["result1", "result2"]]
print(delete_list)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

## 镜像

## 长轴约束

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 限定椭圆的长轴半径具有特定的值

**示例：**

```python
#长半轴约束
pt1 = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
v = NCTI.Vector(0,0,1)
dir = NCTI.Vector(1,0,0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_ellipse","Bellipse", pt1, v, dir, 2, 1)
#create dcm
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_ellipse", "Dellipse", "dcmsys1", pt1, dir, 2, 1, "Bellipse")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmlock1", DCM_LOCK_CENTRE)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "dcmlock1", "Dellipse")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "minor", DCM_MINOR_RADIUS, 1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "minor", "Dellipse")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "dcmLockDir", DCM_LOCK_DIRECTION)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "dcmLockDir", "Dellipse")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "major", DCM_MAJOR_RADIUS, 1)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "major", "Dellipse")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
doc.Rename("Bellipse","result")
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ["result"]]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

## 闭合曲面分割成指定数量的部分

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 根据给定形状中的旋转表面、圆柱、圆锥、球体或环面生成的，它适用于可以闭合（无论是否周期性）的B样条曲面或贝塞尔曲面，并且这些曲面通常具有接缝边缘

```python
maxtol = 0.1

```

## 阵列

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 根据阵列方向、偏移距离、裁剪范围、新增数量，将基础形状阵列成一个新形状

**示例：**

```python
#示例1，长方体阵列
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b", NCTI.Point(-5, -5, -5), 10, 10, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_periodic", "bb", "b", [0, 10, -2, 3], [1, 15, 0, 1], [2, 10, 3, 2])

#示例2，球体阵列
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_sphere", "s", 5, NCTI.Point(5, 0, 0))
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_periodic", "ss", "s", [0, -6, 7, 1], [2, 10, -4, 3])
```

---

## 阵列约束

**类型：** 约束命令

**说明：** 指定行数、列数和间距，结合“固定”约束，创建重复的几何图案

**命令：**

- `doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", strDcmName, strConstraintsTypeName)`

**示例：**

```python
#create model body
geom_list = []
pt = NCTI.Point(0,0,0)
xdir = NCTI.Vector(1, 0, 0)
ydir = NCTI.Vector(0, 1, 0)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline1", pt, xdir)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_line","Bline2", pt, ydir)
geom_list.append("Bline1")
geom_list.append("Bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dim_sys", "dcmsys1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline1", "dcmsys1", pt, xdir, "Bline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_line", "Dline2", "dcmsys1", pt, ydir, "Bline2")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "Dline1")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", "Dline2")
# Add a 2D pattern
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_add_pattern_2d", "patt_2d_gn","dcmsys1", "Dline1", "Dline2")
# Define pattern sizes
xsize = 8
ysize = 5
# Create arrays of points and circles
ptss = []
cirss = []
for ix in range(xsize):
    pts = []
    cirs = []
    for iy in range(ysize):
        BptName = f"Bpt{ix}{iy}"
        DptName = f"Dpt{ix}{iy}"
        pt = NCTI.Point(ix * 30, iy * 15, 0)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_vertex", BptName, pt)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_point", DptName, "dcmsys1", pt, BptName)
        pts.append(DptName)
        geom_list.append(BptName)
        BcirName = f"Bcir{ix}{iy}"
        DcirName = f"Dcir{ix}{iy}"
        r = 5 + (ix + iy) / 2.0
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_circle",BcirName, pt, r)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_circle", DcirName, "dcmsys1", pt, r, BcirName)
        cirs.append(DcirName)
        geom_list.append(BcirName)
    ptss.append(pts)
    cirss.append(cirs)
# Add a concentric between the first point and circle
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "conc", DCM_CONCENTRIC)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_setends", "conc", ptss[0][0], cirss[0][0])
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  "conc", ptss[0][0], cirss[0][0])
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_fix", "dcmsys1", cirss[0][0])
# Add a pattern constraint between the element[0,0] and every other element
for ix in range(xsize):
    for iy in range(ysize):
        dimName = f"pattern{ix}{iy}"
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", dimName, DCM_PATTERNED_2D)
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_edit_dcm_dim_set_pattern_plier", dimName, ix, iy)
        if ix == 0 and iy == 0:
            continue
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  dimName,ptss[0][0], ptss[ix][iy], "patt_2d_gn")
        doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addd", "dcmsys1",  dimName,cirss[0][0], cirss[ix][iy], "patt_2d_gn")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
pvs = doc.dcm_pattern_2d_value("dcmsys1", "patt_2d_gn")
print(pvs)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "pat_value1", DCM_PATTERN_2D_VALUE_1, 40)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_dcm_dimension", "pat_value2", DCM_PATTERN_2D_VALUE_2, 10)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "pat_value1", "patt_2d_gn")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_addr", "dcmsys1",  "pat_value2", "patt_2d_gn")
pvs = doc.dcm_pattern_2d_value("dcmsys1", "patt_2d_gn")
print(pvs)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_dcm_evaluate", "dcmsys1")
pvs = doc.dcm_pattern_2d_value("dcmsys1", "patt_2d_gn")
print(pvs)
con_bal_res = doc.dcm_constraint_balance("dcmsys1")
d_f = con_bal_res[1]
c_f = con_bal_res[2]
print(f"自由度：{d_f}")
print(f"约束度：{c_f}")
print(f"刚性体自由度：{con_bal_res[3]}")
index = 1
result_list = []
for geom in geom_list:
    result_name = f"result{index}"
    doc.Rename(geom, result_name)
    result_list.append(result_name)
    index+=1
names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in result_list]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

## 限制角度分割对象

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_import_file", "CTO900_cts18140-part.stp", "a")
```

## 面片几何替换

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 将指定面片替换为新的面片

**示例：**

```python
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_box", "b", NCTI.Point(0, 0, 0), 300, 200, 100)
faces1 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("b", NCTI.Point(0, 100, 50))
faces2 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("b", NCTI.Point(300, 100, 50))
remove_faces = [faces1[0], faces2[0]]

doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_arc", "a1", NCTI.Point(150, 100, -20), 200, 2.094395, 4.18879)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_prism", "p1", "a1", NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 140))

doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_arc", "a2", NCTI.Point(150, 100, -20), 200, 5.235988, 1.047198)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_prism", "p2", "a2", NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 140))

new_faces = ["p1", "p2"]

doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_face_replace_surface", "result",  "b", remove_faces, new_faces, 0.01)

names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ['result']]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

## 面片变换

**类型：** 建模命令

**说明：** 对指定面片进行几何变换

**示例：**

```python
doc.Clear()
doc.ResetCaseResult()
pnts = [NCTI.Point(0, 0, -150), NCTI.Point(300, 0, -150), NCTI.Point(300, 500, -150), NCTI.Point(200, 500, -150), NCTI.Point(200, 280, -150), NCTI.Point(0, 280, -150), NCTI.Point(0, 0, -150)]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_polyline", "pl", pnts)
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_create_plane", "plane", "pl")
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_prism", "pr", "plane", NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 300))

faces1 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("pr", NCTI.Point(150, 280, 0))
faces2 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("pr", NCTI.Point(250, 500, 0))
faces3 = doc.FindFaceByNearestPoint("pr", NCTI.Point(300, 250, 0))

t1 = NCTI.Transform(NCTI.Vector(0, 100, 0))
t2 = NCTI.Transform(NCTI.Point(300, 500, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 1), 30.0)
t3 = NCTI.Transform(NCTI.Point(300, 500, 0), NCTI.Vector(0, 0, 1), 15.0)

doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_face_transform", "result", "pr", [faces1[0], faces2[0], faces3[0]], [t1, t2, t3], 0, 1, 0, 0)

names = doc.AllNames()
delete_list = [item for item in names if item not in ['result']]
doc.RunCommand("cmd_ncti_delete", delete_list)
```

---

**类型：** 建模命令
